<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> found </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (indices.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> t[ti] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ch) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      found </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ti </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// keep a consecutive run going</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ti; i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> t.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (t[i] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ch) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">continue</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (found </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) found </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">preferWordStarts </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> isBoundary</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(t, i)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">          found </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">          break</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (found </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    indices.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(found)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    ti </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> found </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> indices</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Subsequence fuzzy match. Rewards consecutive runs, word starts and an early first</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * hit; returns null when `query` is not a subsequence of `text`.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> fuzzyMatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">query</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">score</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">indices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[] } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> q</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> query.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toLowerCase</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">replace</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">\s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">g</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">""</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">q) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { score: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, indices: [] }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> t</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> text.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toLowerCase</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // Try anchoring the first character at each word start, keep the best alignment.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> best</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">score</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">indices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[] } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> starts</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> t.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (t[i] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> q[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> isBoundary</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(t, i)) starts.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(i)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> starts) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> indices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> scan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(q, t, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, from) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> scan</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(q, t, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, from)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">indices) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">continue</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> score</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> scoreIndices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(q, t, indices)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">best </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> best.score) best </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { score, indices }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> best</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> scoreIndices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">q</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">t</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">indices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[]) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> run </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  indices.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">forEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">idx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    run </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> idx </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> indices[n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> run </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">    // Gaps between matched characters cost more than where the match begins.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> gap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">min</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(idx </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">6</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">min</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(idx, </span><span <span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> run </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">isBoundary</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(t, idx) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> gap</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (indices[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 3</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (t.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">includes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(q)) score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 6</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> t.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0.02</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> matchItem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">query</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">item</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> CommandPaletteItem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> fuzzyMatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(query, item.label)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (label) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { item, score: label.score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, indices: label.indices }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> extra</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [item.description, item.group, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(item.keywords </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [])]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(Boolean)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> fuzzyMatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(query, s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> m </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">extra.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { item, score: Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">max</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">extra.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> m.score)) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0.6</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, indices: [] }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Key that toggles the palette with ⌘/Ctrl. Pass false to disable. */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Defaults to the locale's "Nothing matches that.". */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> CommandPalette</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
